# R8: actual matched-child rows and an auxiliary-mixture attempt

**UNVERIFIED. No all-depth upper bound or original-target resolution was obtained.**

Allocation `e72c37e67efa7a9a`: one native analytical invocation, two substantive proof lines, mathematical programs/arrays/solvers/formal submissions zero. The parent owns the allocation ledger. This report records exact manual identities and the unpaid terms in the two attempted certificates. It does not assert a counterexample to the U inequality or to the proposed final objective bound.

## 1. Target, fixed hierarchy, and accepted premises

For a predetermined nonnegative word h of length n, the original quantity is

    W_n(h) = sup 2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L ||x_0-x_star||^2),
    x_(i+1) = x_i - (h_i/L) grad f(x_i).

The supremum includes every finite Euclidean dimension, every convex differentiable L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every selected minimizer, and every different start. Write W_n^* = inf_(h>=0) W_n(h), and U_n^ZJ = 1/B_n for the exact original all-integer-split dominant benchmark. The normalization is W = 2R. The original question is whether W_n^*/U_n^ZJ tends to one.

Fix an enclosing integer m >= 1536, and throughout all children use

    lambda = 1+sqrt(2), epsilon = 1/(4m), beta = 1-epsilon,
    c = lambda(1+epsilon), a = beta/c,
    H_0 = (), H_(j+1) = (H_j, 1+a c^j, H_j),
    S_j = sum H_j, C_j = 1+S_j, D_m = 1+2S_m.

Each H_j is the original strictly positive word of length 2^j-1. No child resets epsilon or m. Accepted fact `931b9653ce0f7201` gives B_(2^m-1)/D_m < 47/50. Consequently a full-class bound W_(2^m-1)(H_m) <= 17/(16D_m) for every sufficiently large m would give a strict asymptotic separation, since (17/16)(47/50) = 799/800 < 1. That upper bound is not proved here.

The accepted facts used from `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` are exactly these:

1. For each fixed finite word, the full original supremum equals the supremum over origin-vertex projection-simplex envelopes. This retains every original selected minimizer, or equivalently the closest polar distance for positive scores. It is equality of suprema, not an exact uniformly conditioned lift of each original run.
2. For every nonempty compact convex set K containing zero, every start x, and every d >= 1, the actual same-m H_3 satisfies U_d(T_H3 x) <= U_(d+2S_3)(x), with all projections on one K.
3. Complete Part B provides the actual-child identities reproduced below. Neither positivity of the primitive energy for H_j with j >= 3 nor an all-depth U law is an accepted premise.

Here and below K denotes the projection body; D is a scalar trailing parameter. Put

    P(z) = P_K(z),
    F(z) = max_(g in K) (<z,g>-||g||^2/2),
    U_t(z) = max_(g in K) (2<z,g>-t||g||^2),  t>0.

The actual gradient of F is P. Its full minimum set is the polar cone

    K^polar = {z : <z,g> <= 0 for all g in K}.

For any selected x_star in this cone, U_t(x) <= ||x-x_star||^2/t. Thus a proved all-body U law at d=1 would transfer to the full W supremum using the accepted finite-word universality. None of the manipulations below changes the physical initial radius, terminal, dimension quantifier, or word.

## 2. Common actual parent data and the retained root identity

Consider the actual parent H_(j+1), with child mass s = S_j, capacity C = 1+s, bridge b = 1+k, and k = a c^j. Use N = 2^j-1, and write both complete child histories as

    x_0 = x, x_(i+1) = x_i - h_i g_i, g_i = P(x_i), x_N = v, p = g_N,
    y_0 = w = v-bp,
    y_(i+1) = y_i - h_i f_i, f_i = P(y_i), y_N = y, q = f_N.

The same h_i is used at matched indices in the two actual copies. The notation f_i in this section is a gradient vector, not a function value. The two starts are linked by the displayed physical bridge. They are never varied independently.

For a trailing d >= 1 define

    D = d+2s, h = s+b = C+k, M = D+2h,
    r = P(w/D), q_d = P(y/d), p_0 = P(x/M),
    z_R = [w-y+(d+s)q_d]/D,
    z_P = [x-w+(D+h)r]/M.

In particular r is simultaneously the prefix's terminal auxiliary projection and the right child's initial auxiliary projection. It is one actual point, not two independently selected optimizers.

For any t>0, z in K, and l=P(u/t), define

    Rem(u,t,z) = U_t(u) - [2<u,z>-t||z||^2]
               = t||l-z||^2 + 2<u-tl,l-z> >= 0.

The sign follows from the normal inequality <u-tl,z-l> <= 0. If z is a convex mixture of sampled gradients, the second term is a nonnegative combination of their original same-K normal rows. Put RP = Rem(x,M,z_P), RR = Rem(w,D,z_R), and

    V_L = ||x||^2 - ||v+s p||^2,
    V_R = ||w||^2 - ||y+s q||^2,
    LR = 2s<y,q-q_d> + s^2(||q||^2-||q_d||^2) >= 0.

The last sign follows by comparing the optimizers defining U_1(y) and U_d(y). It does not involve a new physical step. Also define

    Np = <v-p,p-r> >= 0,
    Nr = <w-D r,r-p> >= 0,
    Theta = h[CD-k(C+D+k)]/(D+k),
    J = Theta||p-r||^2 + 2h(1+C/(D+k))Np + 2hC/(D+k)Nr.

The accepted Part B identities, with these actual data, give

    E_R := U_D(w)-U_d(y) = (V_R+LR)/D + RR,               (2.1)
    Psi := U_M(x)-U_d(y) = V_L/M + J/M + RP + E_R.       (2.2)

Neither equality assumes V_L >= 0 or V_R >= 0.

The scalar root coefficient itself remains positive at every level relevant to these attempts. Indeed the exact mass recurrence has the solution

    C_j = v_0 c^j + (1-v_0)2^j,
    v_0 = a/(c-2) = beta/[c(c-2)],  0<v_0<1.

This follows by substitution into C_(j+1)=2C_j+a c^j and C_0=1. Since c(c-2)>lambda(lambda-2)=1>beta, the displayed range for v_0 holds. Hence k/C <= c-2. To bound c, sqrt(2)<10/7 gives

    c < (17/7)(6145/6144) < 5/2;

the final comparison is 208930 < 215040 after multiplication by positive denominators. Thus k<C/2. For j>=2, C>5 and D>=2C-1>9C/5. As in the accepted root calculation,

    Theta/h = C-k-2Ck/(D+k) > C/2-10C/23 = 3C/46,
    Theta > 3C(C+k)/46 > 0.                            (2.3)

Also a>1/3, because beta>9/10 and c<5/2 give a>9/25>1/3; this verifies C_2=4+2a+beta>5. The positive root reserve is therefore available. The unresolved matter is payment of V_L, not the sign of Theta.

## 3. Proof line one: original matched rows, with the actual boundary retained

Set

    delta_i = x_i-y_i, e_i = g_i-f_i, 0<=i<=N.

The exact update is delta_(i+1)=delta_i-h_i e_i. The two original matched normal reserves are

    A_i = <x_i-g_i,g_i-f_i> >= 0,
    B_i = <y_i-f_i,f_i-g_i> >= 0,
    mu_i = A_i+B_i = <delta_i,e_i>-||e_i||^2 >= 0.

Expanding the squared update and summing every physical index gives

    ||delta_0||^2-||delta_N||^2
      = sum_i h_i(2-h_i)||e_i||^2 + 2 sum_i h_i mu_i. (3.1)

The boundary is the actual one:

    delta_0 = sum_i h_i g_i + b p,
    delta_N = sum_i h_i f_i + b p.                    (3.2)

Equations (3.1)-(3.2) retain all matched rows and both complete copies. In particular they do not prove arbitrary two-start nonexpansivity. Already the physical level-two bridge has h_i=1+beta c>2, since beta c=lambda(1-epsilon^2)>1. Thus H_j for j>=3 contains strictly negative coefficients h_i(2-h_i) in (3.1).

For clarity about the boundary terms, put e_N=p-q and define

    Bdy = 2<w,delta_0>
          -2<y+s q,delta_N+s e_N>
          -2s<delta_N,e_N>-s^2||e_N||^2.             (3.3)

Direct expansion of the definitions of V_L and V_R gives

    V_L = V_R + ||delta_0||^2-||delta_N||^2 + Bdy.   (3.4)

This expansion uses x=w+delta_0 and v+s p=y+s q+delta_N+s e_N; hence no endpoint has been substituted. Combining (2.1)-(2.2) with (3.1)-(3.4) yields the exact attempted coupled payment

    M Psi = (M+D)E_R + J + M RP + 2 sum_i h_i mu_i
            + Bdy + sum_i h_i(2-h_i)||e_i||^2
            - LR - D RR.                           (3.5)

If an induction supplied the right-child U law, E_R would be nonnegative. At j=3 that sign is actually supplied by the accepted base. The remaining actual unpaid expression in this construction is

    T_match = Bdy + sum_i h_i(2-h_i)||e_i||^2
              - LR - D RR.                         (3.6)

No estimate proving that J+M RP+2 sum h_i mu_i+(M+D)E_R pays the negative part of (3.6) was obtained. In particular, replacing LR and RR by independent favorable values would reverse their role in (3.5). They are the actual right-child remainders with a negative sign in this attempted transfer. Keeping their correlation is necessary.

### Why a difference-space child certificate is not a payment

All original off-diagonal cross rows remain available. To state their exact orientation, for a in {L,R} set z_i^L=x_i, z_i^R=y_i, g_i^L=g_i, g_i^R=f_i and

    Q_(i,j)^(a,b) = <z_j^b-g_j^b, g_i^a-g_j^b> <= 0.

The formal projection row one might try to assign to the differences is

    S_ij = <delta_j-e_j, e_i-e_j>.

Expansion in the original samples gives exactly

    S_ij = Q_ij^(L,L) + Q_ij^(R,R)
           - Q_ij^(R,L) - Q_ij^(L,R)
           + Q_jj^(R,L) + Q_jj^(L,R)
         = Q_ij^(L,L) + Q_ij^(R,R)
           - Q_ij^(R,L) - Q_ij^(L,R) - mu_j.        (3.7)

Although every Q on the right is nonpositive, the two negative coefficients prevent a conclusion S_ij<=0. Thus the paired update and cocoercivity do not establish that (delta_i,e_i) is a gradient history of one smooth convex function or a projection history of one body. The complete child certificate cannot be applied in that fictitious space. Equation (3.7) explicitly records the extra cross rows that such a step would have to pay; they were not silently discarded.

The sign difficulties in (3.5)-(3.7) are a failure of this proposed proof. They are not an admissible function counterexample to U, and they do not rule out a different nonnegative combination of the full original cross rows.

## 4. Proof line two: mix the actual left auxiliary optimizer into the parent

The second attempt replaces the unpaid V_L by the actual left U gap minus its maximization remainder, and tries to pay that remainder through a feasible parent comparison point. It uses the original same-body optimizers rather than an independently reset child maximizer.

Put

    A = 1+2s,
    l = P(x/A), z_L = [x-v+C p]/A,
    u = l-z_L, n_L = x-A l,
    RL = Rem(x,A,z_L) = A||u||^2+2<n_L,u> >= 0,
    E_L = U_A(x)-U_1(v).

Both z_L and l belong to the same K. Since x-A z_L=v-Cp, one has

    RL = 2<v-Cp,u>-A||u||^2,
    V_L = A(E_L-RL).                               (4.1)

The second equality is the exact child maximization identity at trailing one. If the left child U induction were valid, E_L would be nonnegative; at j=3 this is the accepted base. No sign for V_L is inferred.

For a parameter theta choose the candidate feasible parent point

    z_theta = z_P + (theta A/M)u.

Writing G=x-v=sum_i h_i g_i gives its complete gradient mixture:

    M z_theta = (1-theta)G + (b-theta C)p
                +(D+h)r + theta A l.               (4.2)

The coefficients sum to M. Therefore the following explicit range certifies membership in K using only convexity and the original samples:

    0 <= theta <= min(1,b/C).                        (4.3)

For j>=2, k<C/2 and C>5 imply b<C, so this range has theta<=b/C<1. Equation (4.3) is a sufficient range for this convex-mixture construction; it is not asserted to characterize membership for every particular body. Extending it would require an actual membership proof using the coupled history.

Let R_theta=Rem(x,M,z_theta)>=0 in the range (4.3). The difference of the two feasible variational values is obtained by one square expansion:

    M { [2<x,z_theta>-M||z_theta||^2]
        -[2<x,z_P>-M||z_P||^2] }
      = 2theta A<w-(D+h)r,u> - theta^2 A^2||u||^2.  (4.4)

Use

    w-(D+h)r = v-Cp + (s-k)p-(D+h)r

and (4.1). Combining the result with (2.2) gives the exact same-body identity

    M Psi = A E_L + M E_R + J + M R_theta + T_theta, (4.5)

where the unpaid term is

    T_theta = -(1-theta)A RL
              +theta(1-theta)A^2||u||^2
              +2theta A<(s-k)p-(D+h)r,u>.           (4.6)

Equivalently, using the actual normal n_L,

    T_theta = -(1-theta)^2 A^2||u||^2
              -2(1-theta)A<n_L,u>
              +2theta A<(s-k)p-(D+h)r,u>.           (4.7)

Here <n_L,u> >= 0 is itself the actual maximization normal reserve. Thus it occurs with a negative coefficient in (4.7). The mixed term has no sign from the rows already used. None of E_L, E_R, R_theta, p, r or u may be chosen independently to make it favorable.

The value theta=1 would remove the explicit left-remainder coefficient in (4.6), but (4.2) would give the bridge gradient weight b-C=k-s<0. Convexity of K then does not certify membership of that proposed point. This is a concrete feasibility limitation of this auxiliary mixture, not a claim that z_1 is outside every particular K. At the largest guaranteed feasible theta=b/C, the negative squared coefficient in (4.7) is still strictly present.

One can also write the mixed vector as

    (s-k)p-(D+h)r = (s-k)(p-r) -(D+1+2k)r.         (4.8)

The root square in J controls p-r. Completing a square against it alone leaves the r component in (4.8), along with the signed left normal in (4.7). I did not obtain an original-cross-row inequality controlling that component jointly with E_L, E_R and R_theta. In particular, a completion that treated u and r as free could produce a negative formal direction, but such a direction would not be a realizable counterexample: the actual projections and bridge impose additional constraints. No such formal direction is promoted to a statement about the full U law here.

This second attempt supplies an admissible family of parent comparison points and its exact unpaid term. It supplies neither a nonnegative certificate nor a controlled final loss. An assumption that the remainder in (4.5) is nonnegative would be the missing all-depth argument, not a completed recursion.

## 5. Consequence, audit, and bounded stopping record

The accepted H_3 U law survives both analyses. Its scalar root reserve remains positive at higher levels by (2.3), but that fact alone does not give a sign for either complete unpaid expression (3.6) or (4.6). The direct full-class target W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) therefore remains unproved. No competing full-class lower bound was derived. The original asymptotic minimax question remains OPEN.

The following scope checks were performed manually:

- Every index i in both children is retained, including the actual endpoints; the physical word length is never shortened.
- Both physical paths and every auxiliary optimizer use one fixed K. In particular the right initial r is exactly P(w/D).
- The bridge relation appears in both (3.2) and (4.2)-(4.4). Arbitrary two-start nonexpansivity is not an assumption.
- Difference vectors were not declared to be gradients of a new function. Their exact original-row expansion (3.7) shows the unproved extra sign.
- V_L and V_R are not assumed nonnegative. H_3 primitive positivity and the R7 all-level terminal potential are not premises.
- No old fixed-source fan, Green parametrization, raw array, solver result, or finite table is used.
- Neither a signed quadratic remainder nor an unproved coefficient sign is a function counterexample.
- No approximation, limit interchange, or normalization change is used to infer a full-class bound.

Read completely: the allocation TASK (SHA-256 `4aac7aea7322362f0ce321119a822313a6e842b43c287edfd769e774a2c4082c`), current OPERATOR, original PROBLEM, R7_DIRECT_HANDOFF, R7 relaxed-terminal PROOF_ATTEMPT, the statement and complete Parts B/C of accepted `1e0bf3f41457d8e4`, and the historical PUBLIC `R8_PRIOR_U_RECORD_fd4d21078037120b.md`. The latter's old H_2 gap is superseded by the accepted proof and was not reopened. Its shifted-terminal-norm counterexample was not re-proved. The statement of accepted `931b9653ce0f7201` was read for the precise conditional asymptotic implication. Initial combined read output was truncated; the relevant source portions were re-read in bounded calls before use. The memory registry was consulted only for target/evidence-scope consistency; current source files control every premise.

The mathematical-academic-writing skill and its mathematical-integrity/source-audit references were used to keep proof claims, exact identities, and unsupported signs distinct. No external mathematical theorem is imported into the new derivations, so no literature claim needs a new source.

The two allocated proof lines are exhausted. No mathematical programs, numerical arrays, solvers, formal submissions, further agents/models, paid consults, private worker memory, service/worker actions, truth-store edits, publication, finalization, revocation, shutdown, or Goal mutations occurred. <!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001593 -->
# Handoff: multi-resolvent recursion and zero-aggregate primitive defects

JOINT UNVERIFIED. Fresh guidance `dea2e985f69b1740`.
One analytical invocation, two explicit laws, zero mathematical programs
and zero formal calls. STOP; parent closes the ledger. Original target OPEN.

## Outcome

Both attempted multi-resolvent laws fail as globally PSD original-row
certificates for every actual same-m H_m, m>=1536. A shared complete
formal negative direction is given below. It is not a legal GD history.
The target `U1(T_Hm x)<=17 U_Mm(x)/16` remains open.

## Law 1: actual right suffixes

Use `ell_j=2^m-2^j` and the actual auxiliaries
`a_j=P_D(x_(ell_j)/M_j)`; identify `a_0=g_n`. The input at each auxiliary
is the original physical affine expression divided by M_j. At each join,
use the accepted primitive-left/actual-auxiliary bridge identity with
trailing scale M_j and preserve every normal and maximization square.

REPORT (3.1)--(3.6) specifies the entire nonnegative physical primitive
array, using the positive accepted H2 base and the exact signed join

    F_(j+1)=F_j^left+K_j F_j^right
             +R_j(||g_bridge||²-||g_endpoint||²),
    K_j=(3C_j+k_j)/(C_j-k_j),
    R_j=(C_j+k_j)[C_j²-k_j(2C_j+k_j)]/(C_j-k_j).

Here C_j=1+S_j, k_j=a c^j, and every child uses the same enclosing m.
All original row coefficients are nonnegative; R_j stays signed in the
full residual. REPORT (4.4) gives every new physical--auxiliary row.
The complete residual Fcal_m is REPORT (4.6), with no dropped child
matrix or maximization remainder. Law 1 adds star coefficient
`2 eta M_m` at a_m, eta=1/16, giving

    Rlaw1=Fcal_m+eta M_m||a_m||².

## Law 2: a genuine initial-resolvent chain with exact positive energy

Retain that complete base and introduce `q_j=P_D(x0/M_j)` at all scales.
Identify `q_0=g_0` and `q_m=a_m`. Put

    tau_j=eta M_j/M_m, 0<=j<m,
    sigma_0=tau_0,
    sigma_j=tau_j-tau_(j-1), 1<=j<m,
    sigma_m=2 eta-tau_(m-1).

Replace Law 1's star rule by original coefficients

    lambda_(*,q_j)=M_j sigma_j,
    lambda_(q_j,q_(j+1))=M_(j+1) tau_j.

All are positive and the scaled star budget sums to `2 eta=1/8`.
Every x0 coefficient cancels exactly. With u_j=M_j q_j,

    eta U_Mm(x0)+sum(new lambda Q)
      = E_m
      = eta/(2M_m) [||u_0||²+||u_m||²
                    +sum_(j<m)||u_j-u_(j+1)||²].

This is a proved algebraic identity within the unverified report, not an
assumed payment. Its full law residual is `Rlaw2=Fcal_m+E_m`.

## Exact common obstruction

For m>=6, set physical gradients

    g_16=g_17=g_18=g_19=v,
    g_24=g_25=g_26=g_27=-v,

with v!=0; all other physical gradients are zero. Set x0=0 and preserve
the original affine GD position expressions. Each support has identical
physical mass C_2+k_2, so its signed sum cancels the other support.

These are the left halves of two H3 children inside the right H4 of the
first H5 block. Every positive H2 residual vanishes. The two H3 primitive
residuals are each `R_2||v||²`, where R_2<0 analytically. Their containing
H4 gives `(1+K_3)R_2||v||²`; its containing H5 gives
`K_4(1+K_3)R_2||v||²`. Higher left-child scales are one.

All right-suffix auxiliary inputs are zero after these cancelling blocks;
all initial auxiliary inputs are zero because x0=0. Set a_j=q_j=0. This
respects a_0=g_n, q_0=g_0 and q_m=a_m. Every bridge square, maximization
square and allowance-chain square is zero. Thus the FULL matrices give

    Rlaw1=Rlaw2=K_4(1+K_3)R_2 ||v||²/M_m < 0.

This is a formal PSD obstruction only. The physical assignment has
g0=0 and later nonzero gradients, so it cannot be an actual GD run.

Scope: the two explicit coefficient laws and repairs of this base by
auxiliary-only energy vanishing at the stated zero auxiliary values.
General block entrance/exit auxiliaries, new physical--auxiliary
couplings, joint coefficient redistributions or the desired inequality
are not excluded.

## Optional radial lead checked during closeout

No third law was opened. For g=P_D(z), q=P_D(z/d), d>1, e=g-q, the
parent's lead has the exact original-row identity

    Lrad = [2sd+s²(d+1)]/(d-1) ||e||²
           - [2s(d+s)/(d-1)] Q_(q,g)
           - [2sd(1+s)/(d-1)] Q_(g,q).

At d=1, q=g and Lrad=0. This identity does not establish a tree payment
of the signed primitive defects. Applying it in a new law is outside
this closed two-law invocation.

## Original target and accounting

The full polar minimum set is retained in REPORT Section 8. An eventual
all-body U bound would transfer by accepted finite-word universality to
`W(H_m)<=17/(16M_m)` and then to `liminf W_n^*/U_n^ZJ<=799/800`.
That implication remains conditional.

# R9 fixed finite-law handoff — UNVERIFIED

Date: 2026-10-09. Allocation grantff598f7965f7d469. One native invocation, two substantive lines, zero mathematical programs/arrays/calculators/solvers/formal submissions, zero extra agents or consults. No worker/service, truth-store, publication/finalization/revocation or Goal operation occurred. Parent owns the ledger and closeout.

## Status

Original exact-profile minimax target OPEN. No target-scale law was obtained even for the entire inherited H_m family. No full-class asymptotic upper certificate was obtained. All new mathematics is UNVERIFIED and is fully derived in PROOF_ATTEMPT.md.

Original normalization remains W=2R, with the actual final iterate and ORIGINAL initial radius. B_n is the exact all-integer-split Zhang--Jiang profile. For each enclosing m>=1536, eps=1/(4m), beta=1-eps, c=(1+sqrt2)(1+eps), a=beta/c are held fixed throughout H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j). The supplied accepted comparison is B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50, D_m=1+2 sum H_m.

## Line 1: a real 64-atom uniform match fails

Take equal-probability midpoint atoms of [-h0^2,h0^2], where h0=1+a. Their original variance is h0^4(1-64^-2)/3. A sup-norm comparison with the exactly folded uniform law proves that the first finite output has absolute coordinates below57/64. Every remaining prefix multiplier has magnitude at most1, so the entire remaining actual run stays in the quadratic core.

With P_m=beta^(2^m-1)c^-m, this law's payoff is <9P_m^2, and D_m times its payoff tends to0. Its first projection is genuinely nonvertex; subsequent core collapse explains the failure. This is only a bound on this specific law.

## Line 2: exact inverse laws and their optimized radius debt

For any finite mean-zero next lawY and h>1, d=h-1, select a_*>=max(d,maxY), Z=a_*-Y, and conditional right-branch probabilities omega with E[omega Z]=d. The two preimages are

    X_left=Y-h,  X_right=a_*-h+Z/d.

They have mean0 and actual thresholda_*-h; both map toY by the physical h-step. Their exact original variance payment is

    VarX=VarY+2h a_*-h^2+(d^-2-1)E[omega Z^2].

Backward atom splitting preserves all already-built future laws on one final finite probability space. Every old and terminal projection row is therefore automatic.

An explicit independent-branch law is obtained by starting from terminal±2 and taking a_*=maxY+d and omega=d/a_* at each reversed step. It has at most2^(n+1) positive-weight atoms and multiple active atoms with a strict threshold gap at every preterminal step. Its terminal2F is3. Its endpoints and variance obey

    L_prev=L+h,
    q_prev=(h q+L)/d,
    V_prev=[(q+1/d)/(q+d)]V+h^2 q/d.

This law fails quantitatively: for all inheritedm, its payoff is <(9/8)3^(-2^(m-1)), and D_m times the payoff tends to0. This exposes the radius cost of that policy only; it does not decide optimized inversion.

The allocation AND threshold optimization are closed analytically at one step. Fixed a_* uses a fractional knapsack: fill highestY for1<h<2 and lowestY for h>2, with at most one fractional atom. On each interval with cutoffz and fully selected setA,

    J'(a_*)=d+E[1_A(z-Y)].

For h>=2, the optimized variance is strictly increasing in a_*, so its minimum is at max(d,maxY). For1<h<2 it is convex piecewise affine, with slope

    h^2/d-(d^-2-1)E(Y-z)_+.

The minimum is attained at the lower endpoint or at a feasible upper-tail breakpointa_*=(d+E[Y1_A])/P(A). This is an exact finite selection rule, not a solver result.

At h=sqrt2 the slope-zero stop-loss level is1. If t isY's ACTUAL threshold, p=P(Y>t), and the real guardp(maxY-t)<=sqrt2 holds, choosing the upper tail with a_*=t+sqrt2/p attains

    VarX=VarY+2sqrt2 F(Y)+(2+sqrt2)E[gY^2].

Boundary mass atY=t can create multiple equally optimal thresholds. The guard is material. Perturbation from sqrt2 is NOT required: for every1<h<2, let gamma=(h-1)h/(2-h), choose the actual cutoffz with E(Y-z)_+=gamma and selected upper massp, and set a_*=z+(h-1+gamma)/p. Under the exact guardp(maxY-z)<=h-1+gamma, the attained minimum is

    VarX=VarY+2h gamma F_K(Y/gamma)
             +[h^2/(2-h)]||P_K(Y/gamma)||^2.

The scaling appears only in this identity; physical steps and the original radius are unchanged. The guard bounds the maximum active excess divided by its conditional mean by2/h.

An exact finite tail/gap family was tested with the ACTUAL inherited leafgamma0=h0*a/(1-a)<1. Terminal±2 first inverts to±r, r=2+h0. The level-1 bridge has d=beta and gamma_bridge>r, so its optimized cap inversion makes three atoms

    u=r-(1+beta)+2r/beta, mass beta/(2r),
    v=r-(1+beta), mass1/2,
    w=-r-(1+beta), mass(r-beta)/(2r).

Their stop-loss atv is exactly1. The next leaf's gamma0 cutoff is strictly ABOVEv, with only the upper atom selected. There is therefore no boundary mass to vary at equal cost; this returns to a vertex step. More generally a cap bridge selecting only its minimum atom always regenerates this stop-loss-1 gap. Genuine nonvertex continuation needs multiple distinct selected lower values, or a different boundary-mass family. No propagation of their2/h excess-concentration guard through all inherited levels was proved. This is not a failure theorem for every optimized law.

## A precise obstruction after one-step optimization

For h=2 the globally minimal predecessor variance is

    VarY+4max(1,maxY)-4.

For Y=A with probability1/(A^2+1) and Y=-1/A otherwise, A>=1, both VarY and terminal2F equal1. The optimized predecessor variance is4A-3. Symmetric±1 has the same terminal variance and value but predecessor variance1. Attainment is explicit by splitting the negative atom with conditional right probability1/A at a_*=A.

Thus even optimized inversion cannot be controlled by terminal variance and value alone. This does NOT prove that the optimized policy fails H_m; it rules out a scalar induction that discards tail geometry.

## All-word edge cases and the exact remaining gap

The proof supplies actual inverse formulas for h=0 (identity), 0<h<1 (the unique monotone inverse using E(Y-a_*)_+=1-h), and h=1 (all maximum atoms receive their common least-variance preimage). Hence a completely specified greedy reverse law can be selected for any finite nonnegative word, starting from terminal ±2, with at most n+2 atoms and exact payoff 3/V0.

What is absent is the quantitative boundV0<=3B_n/(1-o(1)), uniformly over all words and all sufficiently large n; it is absent even for inheritedH_m. One-step minimal variance is not known to minimize the whole original radius because different equal-cost inverse choices alter future-to-past tail costs. A completion must control actual maximum support, stop-loss cutoffs, threshold ties and every child-copy multiplicity without resetting laws or radius.

Neither inverse existence nor the sqrt2 equality is the requested lower bound. No complete candidate is ready for formal submission. Stop here; no unused allowance is carried forward.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001595 -->
# R9 fixed-law nonvertex attempt — UNVERIFIED

Date: 2026-10-09. Allocation: grantff598f7965f7d469. One native analytical invocation; two substantive lines; mathematical programs, arrays, calculators, solvers and formal submissions: zero. No child agent, consult, worker/service operation, private worker file, truth-store mutation, publication, finalization, revocation or Goal change was used.

All mathematics in this document is an unverified direct-research draft. It has not entered the Danus fact graph. Current OPERATOR.md, the complete original PROBLEM.md, TASK.md and R8_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md were read. A truncated combined display of PROBLEM.md was repaired by reading its remaining sections separately. The old memory note was used only to locate and preserve the original scope; current project instructions supersede its historical pause status.

## Verdict and original target

No target-scale finite law was obtained. The original problem remains OPEN. Line 1 supplies a fully specified finite nonvertex law but proves that its normalized payoff is negligible on the entire inherited family. Line 2 constructs actual finite laws backwards, solves their one-step branch/threshold variance optimization, and identifies the unresolved distributional estimate. Neither is an upper bound on the whole simplex family or on the full smooth-convex function class.

The target remains the uniform assertion

\[
 \forall\eta>0\ \exists N_\eta\ \forall n\ge N_\eta\ \forall h\in[0,\infty)^n:
 \quad \mathcal W_n(h)\ge(1-\eta)/B_n,
\]

where \(\mathcal W=2\mathcal R\), the denominator uses the ORIGINAL selected minimizer and initial point, and the performance is at the actual last iterate. The benchmark is the exact all-integer-split recursion

\[
C_0=B_0=1,\quad
C_n=\max_{k+l=n-1}\frac{C_k+C_l+\sqrt{C_k^2+6C_kC_l+C_l^2}}2,
\]
\[
B_n=\max_{k+l=n-1}\left(2C_k+\frac{B_l}{2}
 +\frac{\sqrt{B_l^2+8C_kB_l}}2\right).
\]

A negative answer still requires an asymptotic full-function-class upper certificate. No such certificate is given here.

For each enclosing \(m\ge1536\), fix

\[
\epsilon=\frac1{4m},\quad \beta=1-\epsilon,\quad
c=(1+\sqrt2)(1+\epsilon),\quad a=\beta/c,
\]
\[
H_0=(),\quad H_{j+1}=(H_j,1+ac^j,H_j),\quad
n_m=2^m-1,\quad S_m=\sum H_m,\quad D_m=1+2S_m.
\]

The accepted comparison supplied in the task is \(B_{n_m}/D_m<47/50\). Consequently a law with payoff asymptotic to \(1/D_m\) still misses the requested target. The inherited parameters above are kept fixed at every internal level.

## Common actual-function construction

Let a finite probability space have positive probabilities \(p_i\), and put

\[
E=\{X:\mathbb E X=0\},\qquad
K=\{g\in E:g_i\ge-1\},\qquad
F_K(X)=\max_{g\in K}\left(\mathbb E[Xg]-\tfrac12\mathbb E g^2\right).
\]

This is an ordinary Euclidean space under \(X_i\mapsto\sqrt{p_i}X_i\). The set \(K\) is compact: its defining conditions give \(-1\le g_i\le(1-p_i)/p_i\). It is convex, and zero is in its relative interior. The maximizer is the Euclidean projection \(P_KX\). Projection optimality gives

\[
\langle X-P_KX,k-P_KX\rangle\le0\quad(k\in K).
\]

Adding the inequalities for two inputs proves that the projection is 1-Lipschitz. Strong concavity in the maximization variable gives the gradient \(\nabla F_K=P_K\); hence \(F_K\) is globally convex and 1-smooth. It has unique minimum zero at zero: for nonzero \(X\), a sufficiently small positive multiple \(sX\) lies in \(K\), and \(F_K(X)\ge(s-s^2/2)\|X\|^2>0\).

The unique scalar \(t\) satisfying \(\mathbb E(X-t)_+=1\) gives

\[
g=(X-t)_+-1.
\]

Indeed this is the projection's clipped Lagrange-multiplier formula, and its mean is zero. The stop-loss function is continuous and strictly decreasing below the maximum atom, with range from infinity to zero. Moreover \(\mathbb E(X+1)_+\ge1\), so \(t\ge-1\). Writing \(U=(X-t)_+\), one has \(\mathbb EU=1\), \(\mathbb E[Xg]=t+\mathbb EU^2\), and therefore

\[
F_K(X)=t+\tfrac12+\tfrac12\mathbb EU^2
       =t+1+\tfrac12\mathbb Eg^2.                 \tag{1}
\]

Every constructed step below is exactly \(X'=X-hg\). A probability atom can be split into several atoms with identical current and future values: aggregate probabilities, thresholds, moments and future dynamics remain unchanged. Thus backward splitting specifies one finite probability space before the forward run. It does not change the function during that run. All old, cross and terminal projection rows follow from the one actual set \(K\); no selected subset of interpolation rows replaces them.

## Line 1: a finite matched-uniform law falls into the quadratic core

This line tests the uniform first-leaf match and closes its finite-dimensional transfer explicitly. It does not attempt to promote the match into an all-depth witness after the core collapse is seen.

Let \(h_0=1+a\), \(R=h_0^2\), and take exactly 64 equal-probability initial atoms

\[
X_{0,i}=R\left(\frac{2i-1}{64}-1\right),\qquad 1\le i\le64.       \tag{2}
\]

These are fixed before the run, have zero mean and variance

\[
V_0=\frac{R^2}{3}(1-64^{-2}).                                  \tag{3}
\]

For comparison only, a uniform continuous variable on \([-h_0^2,h_0^2]\) has first-step threshold \(t=h_0^2-2h_0\). Its inactive and active branches both map onto \([-ah_0,ah_0]\). Their constant densities add, so the first output is uniform on that interval.

This continuous helper is not the delivered function. Couple (2) to it by equal subintervals. The input error is at most \(R/64\) in sup norm. If two inputs differ by at most \(\delta\), their stop-loss roots differ by at most \(\delta\), and their projected gradients differ by at most \(2\delta\). Their outputs after the first step consequently differ by at most \((1+2h_0)R/64\).

For the inherited parameters, \(1/3<a<1/2\), \(h_0<3/2\), and \(R<9/4\). Thus the actual finite first output satisfies

\[
\|X_1\|_\infty <\frac34+\frac9{64}=\frac{57}{64}<1.             \tag{4}
\]

For completeness, \(c<5/2\) follows from \(\sqrt2<10/7\) and \(\epsilon\le1/6144\); then \(\beta>5/6\) gives \(a>1/3\). The upper bound \(a<1/2\) follows from \(c>2\) and \(\beta<1\).

Every nonempty scalar prefix product of the factors \(|1-h_t|\) in \(H_m\) is at most \(a\). To see this, the complete product in \(H_j\) is

\[
P_j=\beta^{2^j-1}c^{-j},\qquad P_j(ac^j)=a\beta^{2^j-1}\le a.   \tag{5}
\]

The recursive decomposition into the first child, bridge, and right-child prefix proves the claim by induction. Dividing a nonempty full prefix by the first factor \(a\) shows that every prefix of the remaining word has product at most one. Since (4) lies in the quadratic core \([-1,1]\), all subsequent actual gradients equal their inputs. No further face transition occurs. Hence

\[
X_{n_m}=\frac{\prod_t(1-h_t)}{1-h_0}\,X_1,
\quad
\frac{2F_K(X_{n_m})}{V_0}
=\frac{P_m^2}{a^2}\frac{\mathbb EX_1^2}{V_0}
<9P_m^2.                                                        \tag{6}
\]

Here \(V_0>1\) follows from \(h_0>4/3\) in (3), and \(\mathbb EX_1^2<1\) follows from (4). The first projection is genuinely nonvertex: a single active atom of probability \(1/64\) would require its excess above the threshold to be 64, while the whole initial support width is less than \(9/2\). Not all atoms are active, because the initial minimum is below \(-1\). The subsequent all-active motion is exactly what destroys this candidate.

The physical sum obeys

\[
S_m=\frac{a}{c-2}c^m+\left(1-\frac{a}{c-2}\right)2^m-1<c^m-1.  \tag{7}
\]

Indeed \(c(c-2)>1>\beta\), so \(0<a/(c-2)<1\). Equations (5)--(7) give

\[
D_m\frac{2F_K(X_{n_m})}{V_0}
<18\beta^{2(2^m-1)}c^{-m}\longrightarrow0.                       \tag{8}
\]

Thus this explicit 64-atom law fails the target throughout the inherited family. This is a statement about (2), not about every finite law.

## Line 2: actual inverse splitting and radius optimization

### Exact inverse for a step larger than one

Suppose a finite mean-zero next law \(Y\) is already fixed. Let \(h>1\), \(d=h-1\), \(q=\max Y\), and choose

\[
a_*\ge\max(d,q),\quad Z=a_*-Y\ge0,\quad t=a_*-h.
\]

The notation \(a_*\) here is a threshold parameter and is unrelated to the inherited constant \(a=\beta/c\). Choose conditional right-branch probabilities \(0\le\omega(Y)\le1\) such that

\[
\mathbb E[\omega Z]=d.                                         \tag{9}
\]

This is feasible because \(\mathbb EZ=a_*\ge d\). Split each atom into

\[
X_L=Y-h=t-Z,\qquad X_R=t+Z/d.                                  \tag{10}
\]

Use probabilities \(1-\omega\) and \(\omega\) within that atom. Then \(\mathbb EX=0\), \(\mathbb E(X-t)_+=1\), and both branches of the ACTUAL forward step map exactly to \(Y\). Zero branch masses can be omitted. The exact variance is

\[
V(X)=V(Y)+2ha_*-h^2+(d^{-2}-1)J,\quad
J=\mathbb E[\omega(a_*-Y)^2].                                  \tag{11}
\]

This follows by expanding (10), using (9), and using \(\mathbb EY=0\). All original-radius costs are present in (11).

### An explicit finite nonvertex law, with a closed quantitative failure

The following choice is useful for exposing the cost of independent branching; it is not asserted to optimize (11). Fix the terminal two-point law \(Y_n=\pm2\) with equal probabilities, so \(2F_K(Y_n)=3\). At each reversed physical step choose

\[
a_*=q+d,\qquad \omega(Y)=p=\frac{d}{q+d},                       \tag{12}
\]

independently of \(Y\). If the next-law support is \([-L,q]\), its predecessor has exact endpoints and variance

\[
L_{\rm prev}=L+h,\qquad q_{\rm prev}=\frac{hq+L}{d},
\]
\[
V_{\rm prev}=\frac{q+1/d}{q+d}V+\frac{h^2q}{d}.                \tag{13}
\]

Start these recurrences with \(L=q=2,V=4\), and use every physical step in reverse order. This specifies all atoms and weights before execution: atoms are indexed by a terminal sign and a binary branch word, with weight

\[
\frac12\prod_{t=0}^{n-1}p_t^{b_t}(1-p_t)^{1-b_t},
\]

and their initial values are the nested affine preimages (10). Thus there are at most \(2^{n+1}\) atoms, every weight is positive, and the dimension is finite for each actual word. No limiting distribution is involved.

At each query the threshold is \(q-1\). Every left preimage is strictly below it and every right preimage is at least one unit above it. There are multiple distinct active atoms, with the same fixed probability space throughout. This is genuine simultaneous face motion and does not invoke a wholesale switch without a gap.

Nevertheless the original radius is too large. From (13), each reverse step increases \(q\) by at least a factor \(1+1/d\). In \(H_m\), exactly \(K_m=2^{m-1}\) physical steps are leaves, with \(d=a\) and \(h_0=1+a\). Set \(\kappa=h_0/a>3\). Immediately before undoing the first physical leaf,

\[
q_1\ge2\kappa^{K_m-1},\qquad
V_0\ge\frac{h_0^2q_1}{a}\ge2h_0\kappa^{K_m}.
\]

Therefore this actual finite law has

\[
\frac{2F_K(X_{n_m})}{V_0}
\le\frac3{2h_0}\kappa^{-K_m}
<\frac98\,3^{-2^{m-1}},
\]
\[
D_m\frac{2F_K(X_{n_m})}{V_0}
<\frac94\left(\frac52\right)^m3^{-2^{m-1}}\longrightarrow0.      \tag{14}
\]

Unlike Line 1, the terminal payoff here is genuinely on a nonlinear part of the function, and every preceding step has multiple active atoms. The failure is the explicitly paid initial second moment, not an invalid interpolation row. It rules out only policy (12).

### Optimize the allocation, including the threshold

For fixed \(a_*\), (11) is a fractional-knapsack problem with resource \(\omega Z\). Its cost per resource unit is \(Z\). If \(0<d<1\), minimize \(J\) by filling the smallest positive \(Z\), equivalently the largest \(Y\), first. If \(d>1\), the coefficient in (11) is negative, so maximize \(J\) by filling the largest \(Z\), equivalently the smallest \(Y\), first. At most one atom needs a fractional split. Ties may be combined or resolved in the original atom order.

The threshold can also be optimized exactly. On an interval of \(a_*\) where the cutoff atom has value \(z\), let \(A\) be the set of fully selected atoms, with mass \(p_A\), first moment \(\mu_A\), and second moment \(\nu_A\). Directly eliminating the fractional mass gives

\[
J(a_*)=a_*\bigl(d-\mu_A+zp_A\bigr)+\nu_A-z\mu_A-zd,
\]
\[
J'(a_*)=d+\mathbb E[1_A(z-Y)].                                 \tag{15}
\]

For \(d>1\), \(A\) is a lower tail. Since its allocated deficit is at most \(d\),

\[
0\le\mathbb E[1_A(z-Y)]\le\mathbb E[1_A(a_*-Y)]\le d.
\]

The optimized value in (11) is consequently strictly increasing in \(a_*\), with piecewise derivative at least

\[
2h+2d(d^{-2}-1)=2+2/d>0.
\]

Its global minimum is at \(a_* =\max(d,q)\). The same endpoint is optimal for \(d=1\), when the coefficient of \(J\) vanishes.

For \(0<d<1\), \(A\) is an upper tail and (15) gives the variance derivative

\[
\frac{h^2}{d}-(d^{-2}-1)\mathbb E(Y-z)_+.                       \tag{16}
\]

As \(a_*\) increases, the greedy cutoff \(z\) can only move upward. Thus (16) is nondecreasing: the optimized predecessor variance is convex and piecewise affine in \(a_*\). Its slope is eventually \(h^2/d>0\). A minimum is attained at the endpoint \(\max(d,q)\) or at a feasible upper-tail breakpoint

\[
a_*=(d+\mathbb E[Y1_A])/\mathbb P(A),\qquad\mathbb P(A)>0.      \tag{17}
\]

This is a finite exact selection rule, not an instruction to run a solver. If several thresholds minimize the radius, choosing one requires retaining its resulting distribution for subsequent inverse steps. The same current radius does not make those distributions interchangeable.

### The sharp sqrt(2) relation and its actual guard

At \(h=\sqrt2\), \(d=\sqrt2-1\), the stop-loss level where (16) changes sign is

\[
\frac{hd}{1-d}=1.
\]

Let \(t_Y\) be the ACTUAL projection threshold of \(Y\), and let \(p=\mathbb P(Y>t_Y)\). Select those upper-tail atoms and put

\[
a_*=t_Y+h/p.
\]

Then (9) holds. This is feasible precisely when

\[
p(q-t_Y)\le h.                                                  \tag{18}
\]

The other bound \(a_*\ge d\) follows from \(t_Y\ge-1\) and \(p\le1\). With \(U=(Y-t_Y)_+\),

\[
J=(h-2)(a_*-t_Y)+\mathbb EU^2.
\]

Substitution into (11) gives the attained optimized variance identity

\[
V(X)=V(Y)+2\sqrt2 F_K(Y)+(2+\sqrt2)\mathbb E[g_Y^2].             \tag{19}
\]

Boundary mass at \(Y=t_Y\) can create an interval of equally optimal choices of \(p\) and \(a_*\). Guard (18) is necessary: it bounds the maximum active excess relative to its conditional mean. It need not hold for a general core law. Equation (19) is an attained one-step relation, not an all-depth construction.

### Exact inherited-leaf identity and a tested tail/gap family

One need not perturb (19). For ANY \(1<h<2\), put

\[
d=h-1,\qquad\gamma=\frac{dh}{2-h},\qquad
\mathbb E(Y-z)_+=\gamma.
\]

Select the upper tail above \(z\), with total selected mass \(p\), optionally including some mass at \(z\). Put \(a_*=z+(d+\gamma)/p\). The exact feasibility guard is

\[
p(q-z)\le d+\gamma.                                            \tag{19a}
\]

The other bound \(a_*\ge d\) follows from \(z\ge-\gamma\) and \(p\le1\). Writing \(U=(Y-z)_+\) gives

\[
J=(d-\gamma)(a_*-z)+\mathbb EU^2.
\]

Since \((d^{-2}-1)(d-\gamma)=-2h\), the coefficient of \(a_*\) in (11) cancels. When (19a) holds, the attained minimum is exactly

\[
V(X)=V(Y)+2h\gamma F_K(Y/\gamma)
       +\frac{h^2}{2-h}\|P_K(Y/\gamma)\|^2.                    \tag{19b}
\]

The appearances of \(Y/\gamma\) here are only evaluations in a variance identity. The actual physical step remains \(h\), with the original radius and the same function; no run is rescaled. Boundary mass at \(z\) can supply equal-cost choices of tail geometry and should not be dismissed as useless.

For the actual inherited leaf, \(\gamma_0=h_0a/(1-a)<1\), so (19b) applies with the actual parameter whenever its guard holds. The question is whether an exact finite tail family preserves that guard through the intervening bridges.

Here is an explicit test of the single upper atom plus separated lower atoms, allowing a boundary atom. Start backwards from terminal \(\pm R\), with \(R=2\). The first inverse leaf is the optimized symmetric law \(\pm r\), \(r=R+h_0\). To check this directly, the only threshold candidates for symmetric \(\pm R\), with \(R>d\), are \(a_*=R\) and \(a_*=R+2d\); their variance difference is \(2[R(d^{-1}-d)-h^2]\). Thus \(R>\gamma\) chooses the second candidate and gives \(\pm(R+h)\).

The next reversed physical step is the level-1 bridge \(h_b=1+\beta\), with \(d_b=\beta<1\). Its stop-loss level \(\gamma_b=\beta(1+\beta)/(1-\beta)\) exceeds \(r\): here \(\beta>5/6\), \((1-\beta)^{-1}\ge6144\), and \(r<7/2\). Also \(r>\beta\). The same candidate comparison therefore chooses \(a_*=r\), selecting only a fraction of the lower atom. Its exact three-atom output is

\[
u=r-h_b+2r/\beta,\quad v=r-h_b,\quad w=-r-h_b,
\]
\[
\mathbb P(u)=\beta/(2r),\quad
\mathbb P(v)=1/2,\quad
\mathbb P(w)=(r-\beta)/(2r).
\]

It has stop-loss EXACTLY one at the middle atom:

\[
\mathbb P(u)(u-v)=1.
\]

At the next actual inverse leaf, \(\gamma_0<1\), so its cutoff is

\[
z=v+(1-\gamma_0)\,2r/\beta>v.
\]

There is no atom at this cutoff. Only \(u\) is selected; (19a) holds, but there is no boundary mass to vary at equal cost. Its top preimage is \(u-h_0+h_0/\mathbb P(u)\), and every other atom is translated by \(-h_0\). The stop-loss at the translated middle atom becomes \(1+h_0\). Thus this concrete family re-enters a vertex trajectory at the actual leaf, rather than generating the sought simultaneous active-face freedom.

The same regeneration is exact at any later cap bridge that selects only the lowest atom. In particular, for a bridge \(h>2\), if the next law has maximum \(q>d\), minimum \(l\) of mass \(w_l\), and \(w_l(q-l)\ge d\), its greedy lower-tail cap inversion needs only that minimum atom. Its new unique upper atom has mass \(d/(q-l)\), sits \((q-l)/d\) above the largest left preimage, and again gives stop-loss one there. A following inherited leaf with \(\gamma_0<1\) selects only this upper atom. This is a vertex regime already excluded as a new mechanism by the task.

For genuinely nonvertex continuation, a bridge must instead select multiple distinct lower values, or a different family must arrange boundary mass at the actual \(\gamma_0\) cutoff. In the multiple-value case, (19a) requires the excess maximum divided by the conditional excess mean to be at most

\[
\frac{d+\gamma}{\gamma}=\frac2h.
\]

No bound propagating that concentration through all inherited bridges and physical copies was obtained. Nor was an admissible boundary-mass choice found that preserves it recursively while paying the initial variance. This is the precise missing guard for the tested extension; the one-atom calculation is not a proof that every optimized inverse law fails \(H_m\).

An unquantified substitution of (19) at the smaller inherited leaves would still be invalid. There are \(2^{m-1}\) leaves, and any alternative perturbative approach must retain that multiplicity. The exact identity (19b) avoids this perturbation but does not itself propagate its support guard.

### A genuine obstruction after full one-step optimization

Take \(h=2\). Equations (11) and the threshold optimization above give, for EVERY finite mean-zero next law,

\[
\min V(X)=V(Y)+4\max(1,\max Y)-4,                              \tag{20}
\]

where the minimum is over all allowed atom splits and thresholds in the exact inverse construction.

For any real \(A\ge1\), choose the terminal law

\[
Y=A\text{ with probability }\frac1{A^2+1},\qquad
Y=-1/A\text{ with probability }\frac{A^2}{A^2+1}.
\]

It has mean zero and variance one. Its minimum is at least \(-1\), so \(Y\in K\), \(g_Y=Y\), and \(2F_K(Y)=1\). Nevertheless (20) gives minimum predecessor variance \(4A-3\). For the symmetric law \(Y=\pm1\), the same terminal variance and objective give minimum predecessor variance one.

Attainment in the asymmetric example is explicit: set \(a_*=A\); assign right-branch probability \(1/A\) within the lower atom, and use either branch on the upper atom, where \(Z=0\). This satisfies (9). Hence the unbounded discrepancy persists after optimizing BOTH allocation and threshold; it is not an artifact of policy (12).

The conclusion is limited but decisive for a proposed scalar induction: terminal variance and terminal objective alone cannot control even one optimized inverse-radius payment. It does not prove failure of the optimized policy on \(H_m\), and it does not exclude a law family with additional tail control.

### Zero, unit, and subunit steps

The inverse construction can define a fixed finite law for every nonnegative word, including the missing edge cases. These extensions give realizability, not the target bound.

For \(h=0\), take \(X=Y\). For \(0<h<1\), let \(r=1-h\) and choose the unique \(a_*\) satisfying \(\mathbb E(Y-a_*)_+=r\). Set

\[
X=Y-h+\frac h r(Y-a_*)_+,\qquad t=a_*-h.
\]

The map has mean zero and is the actual inverse on each monotone branch. Also \(a_*\ge-r\), so \(t\ge-1\). Its variance is

\[
V(X)=V(Y)+2ha_*-h^2+
 \frac{h(2-h)}{(1-h)^2}\mathbb E(Y-a_*)_+^2.                    \tag{21}
\]

For \(h=1\), write \(a_* =\max Y\), \(p=\mathbb P(Y=a_*)>0\). Take \(X=Y-1\) off the maximum atoms and \(X=a_*-1+1/p\) on them, with threshold \(a_*-1\). Then

\[
V(X)=V(Y)+2a_*-1+1/p.                                         \tag{22}
\]

The common maximum preimage minimizes variance among the possible unit-step preimages by the elementary conditional second-moment inequality. Formulas (21) and (22) retain the same probabilities and original radius.

## What the optimized finite-law attempt does and does not select

One fully definite candidate for any fixed word is now available: begin with the terminal law \(\pm2\), use (21), (22), or the identity for steps at most one, and for steps above one minimize (11) by (15)--(17), choosing the least minimizing threshold and resolving any cutoff tie in atom order. A step above one splits at most one atom, so at most \(n+2\) atoms are needed. Reversing the entire known word once fixes its initial law and its function before the forward run. Its exact normalized payoff is \(3/V_0\).

The requested estimate for this candidate would be

\[
V_0\le\frac{3B_n}{1-o(1)}
\]

uniformly over all words, with a word-independent asymptotic threshold. No such estimate was obtained. Even on the inherited \(H_m\), no closed bound of the form \(V_0\le3B_{n_m}(1+o(1))\) was obtained. The formulas do not establish it. Greedy one-step variance minimization is not known to minimize the original variance across several inverse steps, because its choices change the tail geometry seen by earlier steps.

The missing payment is therefore concrete: control the changing upper support, the stop-loss cutoff and any threshold tie choices of the ACTUAL fixed law through every physical copy of each child, while keeping the accumulated original variance below the exact benchmark scale. Equation (20) proves that replacing those data by variance and objective loses arbitrarily much in general. The actual-parameter identity (19b) still requires propagation of guard (19a); the tested single-upper-atom family regenerates a vertex regime and does not supply that nonvertex propagation. Neither gap is routine or resolved here.

## Scope audit and close

- Every displayed lower-witness function is globally convex and 1-smooth in a finite-dimensional Euclidean space and has attained unique minimum zero. The finite transfer in Line 1 is explicit.
- Each original-radius denominator is the variance of the one initial law, with no restart, independent child law, stepsize rescaling, or new minimizer.
- Formula (14) concerns a specified nonoptimized independent-branch law. Formula (20) concerns one-step optimized inversion and obstructs a particular scalar induction. Neither bounds the supremum over all finite laws.
- The actual inherited parameters and all physical multiplicities are retained. The sqrt(2) identity is not silently applied to smaller inherited leaves.
- The inverse rules cover zero and arbitrary finite nonnegative steps, but no uniform performance theorem follows from their existence.
- There is no complete affirmative or negative original-target candidate to submit for formal review.

The one-invocation allocation is exhausted. Stop after saving these documents; no renewal or further mechanism is authorized by this report.

# Handoff: boundary-clock transport and the fixed-fan obstruction

**JOINT UNVERIFIED. Original target OPEN.** Guidance `1eaef2e700c54cd4`; one fresh `gpt-6-astra` / `ultra` analytical invocation. Two complete laws exhausted; mathematical programs, arrays, solves and formal calls zero. Parent owns registration/closeout.

TASK SHA256: `a042d1e62a2b539ec5c4518d6ce447fc74cf6be384e1864fadd92d1250f91e38`.

## Fixed setting

For enclosing `m>=1536`, all children use `eps=1/(4m), beta=1-eps, c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+eps), a=beta/c, b=1+a`. Let `H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j)`, `D=1+2sum H_m`, `q=17/(16D)`. The prefix deletes only the final update, retaining query `l=2^m-2`.

The task-fixed source is `mu=2pi^(m)`, `nu=0`, where `u^(j)=H_j/S_j`, `alpha=1/(2c)`, `gamma=1/sqrt(c)`, `zeta=1-alpha-gamma`, and `pi^(j)=(alpha u^(j-1),zeta,gamma pi^(j-1))`, `pi^(1)=(1)`. The base fan is `lambda0_ij=1_(i=l)mu_j` for distinct queries, with minimizer rows `lambda_(i,star)=mu_i`, `lambda_(star,i)=0`.

Every original row remains. The complete full matrix is defined by `N_ij=-lambda_ij`, `N_ii=mu_i+sum_(j!=i)lambda_ij`, original chronological `L`, `B=sym(NL)`, and

`Kres=B-a^2 e_l e_l^T-mu mu^T/(4q)`.

No sharp contact, discarded gradient, diagonal remainder or primitive-energy shortcut is imposed. Actual last-step descent remains `2f_after<=2f_l-(1-a^2)||g_l||^2`.

## The two concrete laws

At every nonleaf dyadic node, choose left/right probabilities proportional to `h_i t_J(i)^s`, where `t_J(i)=1+sum_(j in J,j<i)h_j`. The last deleted update's `h_l=b` is only a proof weight, never a GD update in `L`. Add the exact circulation bridge→left→right→bridge, with coefficients `theta pL`, `theta pL pR`, `theta pR`, to the fixed fan. Every duplicate original row is added. This proves nonnegative coefficients and exact value flow.

- **Law 1:** `s=1`, `theta_v=mu_(r_v)^2/(4q)`. The complete query-zero outgoing mass is the sum over ALL leftmost ancestors. Every proper ancestor contributes more than `1/(160H)`, where `H=S_(m-1)`. Thus `Kres_00<-3/H<0` at all stated depths. The root order mismatch has changed, but accumulated traffic still fails.
- **Law 2:** `s=2`, `theta_v=[mu_(r_v)^2/(4q)-mu_(r_v)]_+`. The report proves `Z2(H_d)>S_d^3/4`, treats the inactive depth-two ancestor, and bounds every proper ancestor plus the root. This gives `out_0<1/(3H)` and `Kres_00>2/(125H)>0`. Nevertheless root saturation gives `Kres_rr=0`, while exact original mixed coefficients give `Kres_0r>1/1000`. The 2x2 principal minor is negative.

The second result is a full mixed-matrix obstruction after the summed first-query screen passes. No third repair was investigated.

## Stronger common necessary cut: the whole positive-addition class fails

Main proposed a common cut during this same call; it was independently checked and is proved in REPORT Section 8. It is not a third coefficient law.

Let `C_ij>=0` be ANY zero-query-divergence addition to the fixed fan, not necessarily dyadic. Set `N_C=diag(Ce)-C`, `B_C=sym(N_C L)`. At the global left set `I=[0,r)`, `z=1_I`, `H=sum_(i<r)h_i`, `R=h_r`, `t=L e`,

`z^T B_C z=-sum_(j outside I,i in I) C_ji(H-t_i)`,

`(B_C)_rr=sum_(i<r)C_ri-(R-1)sum_(j>r)C_rj`.

Since `H-t_i>=a`, these imply `z^T B_C z+a(B_C)_rr<=0`. The base contributes `sum_(i<r)mu_i t_i+a mu_r`. With `A=sum_(i<r)mu_i=1/c`, `u=mu_r=2zeta`, PSD would therefore require

`(A^2+a u^2)/(4q) <=sum_(i<r)mu_i t_i+a u`.

The fixed source gives right side `<5H/24+37/60`. Independently, `D>24H/5`, `A>2/5`, `a>2/5`, and `u>17/60>7/25` give left side `>27H/125`. Thus

`z^T Kres z+a Kres_rr <37/60-23H/3000 < -H/150 <0`.

This excludes every nonnegative balanced query addition to this same fan/source, for every `m>=1536`, whatever the landing weights or intensities. It includes both laws above and all allowed positive dyadic circulations. No further boundary-weight tuning inside that class can fix it.

## Exact escape boundary and scope

The common proof requires entrywise retention of the base fan `lambda0_(l,i)=mu_i`, fixed source `mu`, and `nu=0`. It does NOT exclude a reselected nonnegative original array whose difference from the fan has negative entries on some old fan edges, or a changed source/minimizer choice. With the same source, a successful certificate must reduce at least one old terminal-fan edge; simply adding another circulation is excluded.

This boundary is stronger than the predecessor's proportional-landing-only failure. All negative directions are formal Gram directions, not legal GD functions. No full-class upper bound, exact interpolable counterexample, or original-goal resolution is claimed. The two laws and common-cut proof are JOINT UNVERIFIED pending any separately authorized review.

REPORT contains the complete constants, all-ancestor accounting, full original coefficients, quadratic normalization proof and corrected `N^T` convention. No program or raw array was used. Parent closes the analytical ledger. **STOP; original target OPEN; no renewal.**

# R4 handoff: original-flow reselection with full corridor payments

**JOINT UNVERIFIED. Original target OPEN.** Guidance `d776a946dfa9f071`; one fresh `gpt-6-astra` / `ultra` analytical call. Two complete laws exhausted, program/array/solve/formal counts zero. Parent owns registration and closeout.

TASK SHA256: `5c4c44ffcdf442f830a0d31451b92c40edc9f85d2d9d47e4438a0886842fad0d`.

## Fixed coordinates and source

All children keep the same enclosing `m>=1536` parameters `eps=1/(4m), beta=1-eps, c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+eps), a=beta/c, b=1+a`. The prefix deletes only the last update, retaining final query `l=2^m-2`. The target is `q=17/(16D)`, `D=1+2sum H_m`, for `2f_l-(1-a^2)||g_l||^2` at the original radius.

The source remains `mu=2pi^(m)`, `nu=0`, with the task's `alpha=1/(2c)`, `gamma=1/sqrt(c)`, `zeta=1-alpha-gamma`, `pi^(1)=(1)`, `pi^(j)=(alpha H_(j-1)/S_(j-1),zeta,gamma pi^(j-1))`. At the full root `k=2^(m-1)-1`, `H=S_(m-1)`, `e=k-1`, one has `A=sum_(i<k)mu_i=1/c`, `u=mu_k=2zeta`, and `mu_i=h_i/(cH)` inside the left block.

All final original multipliers are nonnegative and have exact value flow. The complete matrix is always the original `N,L,B=sym(NL)`, with `K=B-a^2 e_l e_l^T-mu mu^T/(4q)`; no gradient, mixed term or original row is removed.

## Necessary rerouting independently checked

For any PSD certificate with this source, the original root cut requires

`sum_(i<k)(H-t_i)(mu_i-lambda_(l,i))_+ >H/150`,

and hence `sum_(i<k)(mu_i-lambda_(l,i))_+ >1/150`. REPORT Section 2 derives this from the exact original cut and its uniform scalar deficit. It is necessary, not sufficient.

## Changed base and complete payments

The common absorbing relay has, at every nonleaf dyadic interval `I`, edges `e_I -> e_(I_L)` with mass `A_I=sum_(i in I_L)mu_i`, and `e_I -> r_I` with mass `mu_(r_I)`, recursively in both children. Duplicate rows are summed; singletons absorb their own source through their minimizer row. The exact subinterval query divergence is `M_I e_(e_I)-mu|_I`.

The old terminal fan is actually replaced: at the root, only `lambda_(l,e)=A` goes directly into the left block. Both final laws remove more than `1/3` of old terminal-left weight and more than `H/6` in the weighted removal test. They are not positive additions to the old fixed fan.

Define the explicit balanced adjacent corridor `Corr(s,t;T)`: for `s<=i<t`, add in BOTH directions of `(i,i+1)` the weight

`J_i=T product_(r=i+1)^(t-1)(h_r-1)`.

It adds `+T` at its terminal gradient diagonal, `-T product_(r=s)^(t-1)(h_r-1)` at its initial diagonal, and zero at every interior diagonal, with all adjacent mixed terms retained.

Both complete laws add the terminal corridor `Corr(0,l;T_star)`, where `T_star=a^2+mu_l^2/(4q)<6`. This makes `K_ll=2` exactly. At every nonleaf node use the fully defined intensity

`T_I=[mu_(r_I)^2/(4q)-mu_(r_I)]_+ +q`.

The two laws are:

1. Add `Corr(e_(I_L),r_I;T_I)`, a single adjacent pair at each node.
2. Add `Corr(min I,r_I;T_I)`, traversing the entire actual left block at each node.

The relay alone was audited as a base, not counted as a third law. All completions are nonnegative balanced additions to the NEW base. Both final arrays pass the actual aggregate root test `z^T Kz+aK_kk>H/17`, and both have the derived root margin `K_kk=q>0` and terminal margin `K_ll=2`.

## Decisive exact failures

**Law 1:** Every first-query contribution is included. Only the depth-two node pair and the global terminal corridor touch query zero, giving `K_00>1/(100H)>0`. But the last root-left receiver has exactly `B_ee=A-a T_root<-D/340<0`. Proper local pairs do not touch that receiver, and the global terminal corridor cancels at its interior diagonal. This is a full original-matrix negative direction.

**Law 2:** The full first diagonal retains all leftmost-ancestor losses and the global terminal loss:

`K_00=mu_0-mu_0^2/(4q)-sum_(j=2)^m T_j P_(j-1)-T_star P_pre`.

The exact product is `P_j=beta^(2^j-1)c^(-j)`, `P_pre=P_m/a`. The actual depth-four node `[0,15)` alone has `T_4 P_3>3/(10H)`, forcing `K_00<-11/(320H)<0`. No ancestor cost or mixed term was discarded from the matrix definition.

These formal negative directions need not satisfy smooth-convex interpolation and are not GD function counterexamples. The proofs do not exclude arbitrary original-flow reselection or the fixed source itself.

## Exact algebra retained for the direct-proof priority

The complete original-row corridor identity is

`sum_(i=s)^(t-1) J_i(Q_(i,i+1)+Q_(i+1,i))`

`=T||g_t||^2-T product_(r=s)^(t-1)(h_r-1)||g_s||^2`

` +sum_(i=s)^(t-1)J_i(h_i-2)<g_i,g_(i+1)>`.

This retains all signed mixed terms and can be inserted literally into an independently developed all-depth argument. It is not a positive energy or a convergence proof by itself. The exact absorbing-relay divergence identity above is also available as algebra, not an upper bound. No claim that either identity closes the Zhang–Jiang comparison is made.

Following the latest steering, no further cosmetic routing variant, equivalent program, finite toy experiment or third law is proposed. REPORT gives the complete coefficients, all-depth constants, checks of the original terminal and ancestor traffic, and both scoped failures. Original target **OPEN**. Parent closes this analytical ledger. **STOP; no renewal.**

# Handoff: hierarchical prefix-source transport

**JOINT UNVERIFIED; original target OPEN.** One fresh invocation under `b3302909c64e1914`; exactly two laws exhausted. No third law or new computation is authorized by this handoff.

## Extraction receipt

The one fresh deterministic extraction read the three saved complete `P3/P4/P5` cases. Zero solves, no fit or repair, 20 precharged accounted seconds, `1.2740811170006054` actual whole-process seconds; exit zero, no timeout, complete exports. Numerical credit was closed before the explicitly authorized manual followthrough from SAVED TEXT only. Native registration occurred exactly once. All 142 frozen inputs and old packet bytes were unchanged.

TASK SHA: `f5d0e721873afaee10e7ea18b118393281fa79db4cc2f9960e7a5d0cbe3efcff`.

Freeze SHA: `5b45d7adb081bc7ee5eb347849bd061c5cc6197a649092ebf8bf904c24879f38`.

The saved root source fractions `(left,bridge,right)` were:

- P3: `(0.19024130887437668,0.2113440320897584,0.598414659035865)`.
- P4: `(0.201739473500936,0.16672259731484662,0.6315379291842174)`.
- P5: `(0.2086617859130806,0.14964123771884968,0.6416969763680697)`.

These are numerical shapes, not an exact law. All source/dual/Gram defects remain. Full mu, nu, all row labels/coefficients, N, B, source and direct Schur matrices, spectra and flow corrections are in the new `raw/extraction/<case>/` text and NPZ exports. No old/raw arrays were reloaded after the one extraction.

## Pre-freeze source correction

For `B=sym(NL)`, sharp Huber contact with `nu=0` gives

`N(Le)+2L^T e_l-2a^2 e_l=K_H mu`,

not `N(Le)+L^T mu-2a^2 e_l=K_H mu`. The correction follows from `N^T e=2e_l`. Main confirmed and corrected the lead before freeze; corrected SHA `331b021ab35eddb713798aae2fdc8b064f211e76b8f9d0a9638bbd27b8c843d2`. The original bytes remain snapshotted. Huber's lower witness `1/(D-(1+a)^2)` is unchanged. Sharp contact restrictions are not imposed on the relaxed target.

## Common source law and full matrix

For every enclosing `m>=1536`, all children keep `eps=1/(4m), beta=1-eps, c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+eps), a=beta/c, b=1+a`. Let `u^(j)=H_j/S_j`, a probability on all original full-word step/query labels. Define

`alpha=1/(2c)`, `gamma=1/sqrt(c)`, `zeta=1-alpha-gamma`,

`pi^(1)=(1)`, `pi^(j)=(alpha u^(j-1), zeta, gamma pi^(j-1))`,

`mu=2pi^(m)`, `nu=0`, `q=17/(16D)`.

This distinguishes physical-clock left blocks from recursively biased right-spine blocks. It changes at every right-spine level and is not a first/final correction of the old raw clock. It passes the earlier necessary source bound `mu_0<4q` and central profile cut.

Every law fixes original multipliers, then `N_ij=-lambda_ij`, `N_ii=mu_i+sum_(j!=i)lambda_ij`, `L_ii=1,L_ij=k_j` for `j<i`, and `B=sym(NL)`. The full original residual is

`Kres=B-a^2 e_l e_l^T-mu mu^T/(4q)`.

No rank, diagonal, primitive, common-column or sharp-contact restriction is used.

## Two completed coefficient laws

Both use base transport `lambda^0_ij=1_(i=l)mu_j` for distinct queries and minimizer rows `lambda_(i,star)=mu_i`, `lambda_(star,i)=0`. The base plus star rows has exactly the target value flow.

**Law 1:** For every dyadic interval `I_v`, add symmetric query circulation `tau_v mu_i mu_j/M_v` on all distinct `i,j in I_v`, where `M_v=sum_(i in I_v)mu_i` and the frozen intensity is `tau_v=1+S_(depth v)`. Coefficients are nonnegative and flow remains exact. At the full root, let `H=S_(m-1)`, physical bridge `R`, source masses `A=1/c`, `u=2zeta`, `C=2/sqrt(c)`. Then

`B_kk=u+(tau_root u/2)[A-(R-1)C] <=u`,

so the complete Schur diagonal satisfies `Kres_kk<-D/136<0`. This excludes even arbitrary nonnegative intensities in the same symmetric-product family.

**Law 2:** For each nonleaf node, with source masses `A_v,C_v` on its left/right and bridge `r_v`, take `theta_v=mu_(r_v)^2/(4q)` and add

`r_v -> i in left: theta_v mu_i/A_v`,

`i in left -> j in right: theta_v mu_i mu_j/(A_v C_v)`,

`j in right -> r_v: theta_v mu_j/C_v`.

Each node circulation has exactly zero divergence and nonnegative original coefficients. It repairs the root Schur diagonal to `Kres_kk=u>0`. But it forces `B_00<-21/1700<0` at every requested depth. No PSD completion exists for these exact coefficients.

Even arbitrary nonnegative intensities cannot repair the same routing: query zero requires `theta_root<=1/beta<4/3`, while root PSD requires `theta_root>D/68-2/5>4/3`.

## Load-bearing hypothesis requested by main

The query-zero overload SPECIFICALLY uses the within-left landing/throughput rule

`mu_i/A=h_i/H`, including query-zero fraction `b/H`.

The required root circulation is of order `D`; this landing law gives the first query a fraction of order `1/D`, hence order-one outgoing load. Its exact original row allows outgoing mass at most `mu_0/a`, of order `1/D`. Changing only cycle intensity is excluded by the two incompatible inequalities above.

A different return/throughput distribution with stronger suppression at the initial boundary is not covered automatically. Main mentioned a possible clock-weighted successor; it was not investigated or counted as a third law. The present proof does not exclude that successor, all hierarchical measures, or the source profile with a different full original-row construction.

## Scope and closeout

Both negative directions are formal Gram directions, not realizable GD trajectories. No actual function violates the target in this report. A probability measure without a compatible original-row/PSD construction is not an upper proof; the all-depth weighted-prefix target and original minimax question remain OPEN.

`REPORT.md` contains the complete all-depth definitions, exact flow and PSD-failure proofs, numerical defects and scope. `DERIVATION.md` and `EXTRACT_SCHEMA.md` were frozen before extraction. `CLOSED.json` closes numerical credit; final `budget.json` and `NATIVE_CLOSED.json` close the invocation after two-law followthrough. Remaining jobs/seconds are zero, solves and formal attempts zero. No raw arrays or further mathematical programs after numerical closure. **STOP; no automatic renewal.**

# R12b Profile C handoff

**UNVERIFIED draft; grant 07d388b77ab325df. Two approaches exhausted. STOP. Original target and Profile C remain OPEN.**

There is no full PSD certificate and no actual complete-word Profile C counterexample. PROOF_ATTEMPT.md gives complete derivations for the exact failures below. Parent owns all shared administration and any future allocation.

## Locked object

For one enclosing m>=1536, keep epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon, c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon), a=beta/c and H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a c^j,H_j), with the same parameters in all children. Let S=sum H_m, T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, Bcert=1+(32/17)S, rho_i=1-T_i/(8S), u_i=rho_i h_i physically, and u_n=Bcert-sum_(i<n)u_i. rho_0=1 and rho_n=7/8. ell_n=u_n/rho_n is algebraic only.

The original objective keeps W=2R, the exact all-integer Zhang--Jiang benchmark B_n=2S_bullet(n)+1, all finite-dimensional smooth-convex functions, the original selected minimizer/radius, and the actual terminal iterate. Bcert is not B_n.

## Attempt 1: fixed unequal-cap saturated trajectory is infeasible

The legal function has gradient clip(x,[-A,1]) for a fixed A>0 and unique minimizer 0. Every Q_star vanishes. The proposed alternating starts were

    X_1^+=beta, X_1^-=A beta,
    L_j=s_(j-1)+1+k_(j-1),
    X_j^+=L_j-X_(j-1)^-, X_j^-=A L_j-X_(j-1)^+.

An actually saturated positive left child/bridge requires X_(j-1)^-<=k_(j-1); a negative one requires X_(j-1)^+<=A k_(j-1). Exact formulas and uniform enclosing-m limits give

    X_j^+/s_j -> (c0-A)/(c0+1),
    X_j^-/s_j -> (A c0-1)/(c0+1), k_j/s_j -> r,
    c0=1+sqrt(2), r=sqrt(2)-1.

At the positive root and its actual negative right child these constraints force A<=1 and A>=1. Any fixed A!=1 therefore violates an actual bridge requirement for every sufficiently large enclosing m. The case A=1 returns the old symmetric orbit that passes the supplied Profile C calibration. No terminal values or D_u sign are claimed for the infeasible table. This does not exclude interior or other dwell phases, A depending on m, or other actual functions.

## Attempt 2: exact cyclic matrix exists but its full R fails

Write U=sum physical u_i, b_i=u_i physically and b_n=-U. For any admissible C, define P_i=sum_(j<i)C_ij and F_i=sum_(j>i)C_ij. Including the full covariance,

    R_ii=u_i(2-u_i)+2P_i-2(h_i-1)F_i.

An acyclic nonnegative flow has each outgoing total <=U, because n is the sole net source. A topological-cut proof is in the attempt. At the central bridge u_i is a fixed positive fraction of S, while U<=S, so every acyclic C has R_ii<0 for all sufficiently large m. Genuine circulations are necessary.

The explicit construction tested beyond that class is

    E=sum ell_i=S+ell_n, q_i=ell_i/E,
    p_i=ell_i-b_i, C=p q^T-diag(p).

Here p_i=(1-rho_i)h_i>=0 physically, p_n=ell_n+U>0, sum p=E, and sum q=1. Thus row sums are zero, column sums are exactly b, and every off-diagonal entry is nonnegative. All indices 1,...,n have genuine two-way cycles.

At index 0, K_0j=0, p_0=0, R_00=h_0(2-h_0)>0, and R_0j=(h_0-1)p_j h_0/E. The covariance degeneracy was explicitly retained.

At the central query k=2^(m-1)-1, put s=s_(m-1), hmid=1+k_(m-1), S=2s+hmid. The exact symmetric-part diagonal is

    H_kk=(2p_k/E)[(hmid-1)(s+ell_n)-s]>0.

For every task depth hmid>3, rho_k>15/16, and u_k>2. Hence the FULL remainder has

    R_kk=u_k(2-u_k)-H_kk<0.

This excludes that actual cyclic law at every m>=1536. The coordinate test is not asserted to be an actual trajectory and does not exclude arbitrary C or disprove Profile C. A remaining C must meet P_k-(hmid-1)F_k>=u_k(u_k-2)/2, of order S^2, while also satisfying all other full-matrix conditions. No such C was found.

## Recovery boundary

No fixed Profile A/B coefficient work was repeated. No separate sign assumption was imposed on R-K. No higher primitive positivity was invoked, no child surplus discarded, no child parameters refreshed, and no radius or objective changed. The original-target separation remains conditional on a full-class upper certificate that is still absent.

Do not formally submit this as a Profile C certificate or counterexample. No complete original-target candidate exists for audit. Future work requires a fresh grant and either a substantively different cyclic coefficient law with a full PSD proof, or a fully executed different actual trajectory.

Resource counts: native invocation 1; analytical approaches 2; mathematical programs/arrays/solvers/formal submissions 0 each; additional agents/models 0; worker/service/Goal/truth-store changes 0. Only these two Markdown outputs were written. Parent owns the ledger. Exact per-call token use is unavailable and is not invented.
